CheckpointListCallback(TrainerCallback):\n    \"\"\"Records paths of saved checkpoints for downstream evaluation.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.saved_checkpoints: List[str] = []\n\n    def on_save(self, args, state, control, **kwargs):\n        ckpt_dir = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f\"checkpoint-{state.global_step}\")\n        self.saved_checkpoints.append(ckpt_dir)\n train_dataset: PackedDataset for this generation.\n        config: PilotConfig.\n        model_name_or_path: Override model init (default: config.model_name).\n\n    Returns:\n model_name,\n        torch_dtype=\"auto\",\n        attn_implementation=\"flash_attention_2\" if config.bf16 else \"eager\",\n    )\n    if config.gradient_checkpointing:\n        model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\"use_reentrant\": False})\n\n    ckpt_cb = CheckpointListCallback()\n\n    run_name = f\"{config.wandb_run_prefix}_G{gen_idx}\"\n    training_args = TrainingArguments(\n        output_dir=output_dir,\n        max_steps=config.max_steps,\n        per_device_train_batch_size=config.per_device_batch_size,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=config.gradient_accumulation_steps,\n        learning_rate=config.learning_rate,\n        warmup_ratio=config.warmup_ratio,\n        weight_decay=config.weight_decay,\n m[\"vocab_coverage\"], m[\"eval_time_sec\"],\n        )\n\n        del model\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    return all_metrics\n\n\ndef save_metrics(metrics: list[dict], path: str):\n logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"PILOT PIPELINE START\")\n    logger.info(\"  Model: %s\", config.model_name)\n    logger.info(\"  Generations: %d | Tokens/gen: %dM\", config.num_generations, config.tokens_per_generation // 1_000_000)\n    logger.info(\"  Checkpoint interval: %dM tokens (%d steps)\", config.checkpoint_interval_tokens // 1_000_000, config.save_steps)\n    logger.info(\"  Device: %s\", device)\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # --- Prepare data ---\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(config.model_name)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n\n    logger.info(\"Preparing real data ...\")\n    train_dataset, holdout_dataset = prepare_real_data(config, tokenizer)\n\n    all_results = []\n    prev_gen_token_ids = None\n    prev_model_dir = None  # G0: None → config.model_name; G1+: previous gen's final checkpoint\n    g0_baseline = None  # populated logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # --- Determine training data ---\n        if g == 0:\n            gen_train = train_dataset\n        else:\n            synth_path = config.synthetic_data_path(g - 1)\n            gen_train = prepare_synthetic_data(synth_path, config, tokenizer)\n\n        # --- Train (chain model from previous generation) ---\n        final_model_dir, ckpt_paths = train_generation(\n            gen_idx=g,\n            train_dataset=gen_train,\n            config=config,\n            model_name_or_path=prev_model_dir,\n        )\n\n        # include final model boundary)\n        last_gen_metrics = [m for m in all_results if m[\"generation\"] == config.num_generations - 1]\n        kl_value = last_gen_metrics[-1].get(\"kl_unigram\", 0) if last_gen_metrics else 0\n        kl_ci = (last_gen_metrics[-1].get(\"kl_ci_lower_95\", 0), last_gen_metrics[-1].get(\"kl_ci_upper_95\", 0)) if last_gen_metrics else (0, 0)\n\n        spectral_leads = (first_rank_drop_idx is not None and\n                          (first_ppl_rise_idx is None or first_rank_drop_idx < first_ppl_rise_idx))\n        kl_above_threshold = kl_value > 0.1\n\n        logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n        logger.info(\"MVP GO / NO-GO CHECK\")\n        logger.info(\"  G0 baseline: rank=%.1f +/- %.2f (1-sigma threshold=%.1f)  ppl=%.2f\",\n                     g0_rank_mean, sigma, rank_threshold, g0_ppl)\n        logger.info(\"  PPL significance: >= %.2f (abs +2) OR >= %.2f (rel +5%%)\",\n                     ppl_abs_threshold, ppl_rel_threshold)\n        logger.info(\"  First rank drop (>= 1 sigma): %s\",\n                     f\"checkpoint {first_rank_drop_idx}\" if first_rank_drop_idx is not None else \"NOT DETECTED\")\n        logger.info(\"  First PPL rise (significant): %s\",\n                     f\"checkpoint {first_ppl_rise_idx}\" if first_ppl_rise_idx is not None else \"NOT DETECTED\")\n        logger.info(\"  Spectral leads PPL: %s\", spectral_leads)\n        logger.info(\"  KL divergence: %.4f  (95%% CI: [%.4f, %.4f])  threshold 0.1: %s\",\n                     kl_value, kl_ci[0], kl_ci[1], \"PASS\" if kl_above_threshold else \"FAIL\")\n\n        if spectral_leads and kl_above_threshold:\n            verdict = \"GO — spectral rank is a leading indicator, KL confirms non-stationarity\"\n        elif first_rank_drop_idx is not None and first_ppl_rise_idx is not None and first_rank_drop_idx == first_ppl_rise_idx:\n            verdict = \"CONDITIONAL GO — spectral and PPL drop simultaneously, check granularity advantage\"\n        elif kl_value < 0.1 and (first_rank_drop_idx is not None or first_ppl_rise_idx is not None):\n            verdict = \"EXTEND — KL below threshold but collapse signals detected, add generation to accumulate KL\"\n        elif first_rank_drop_idx is None and first_ppl_rise_idx is not None:\n            verdict = \"KILL — PPL rose but spectral rank did not drop, indicator ineffective for autoregressive LM collapse\"\n        elif first_rank_drop_idx is None and first_ppl_rise_idx is None:\n            verdict = \"EXTEND — neither spectral rank nor PPL moved in 2 generations, add generation to trigger collapse\"\n        else:\n            verdict = \"INCONCLUSIVE — review trajectories manually\"\n\n        logger.info(\"  VERDICT: %s\", verdict)\n        logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        summary[\"mvp_check\"] = {\n            \"g0_rank_mean\": g0_rank_mean,\n            \"g0_rank_std\": sigma,\n            \"rank_threshold_1sigma\": rank_threshold,\n            \"ppl_threshold_abs\": ppl_abs_threshold,\n            \"ppl_threshold_rel\": ppl_rel_threshold,\n            \"first_rank_drop_checkpoint\": first_rank_drop_idx,\n            \"first_ppl_rise_checkpoint\": first_ppl_rise_idx,\n            \"spectral_leads\": spectral_leads,\n            \"kl_unigram\": kl_value,\n            \"kl_ci_95\": list(kl_ci),\n            \"kl_above_0_1\": kl_above_threshold,\n            \"verdict\": verdict,\n        }\n        with open(summary_path, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump(summary, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Collapse-aware mixing pilot experiment\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, default=\"./output\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num_generations\", type=int, default=3)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--tokens_per_generation\", type=int, default=500_000_000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint_interval_tokens\", type=int, default=50_000_000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--model_name\", type=str, default=\"EleutherAI/pythia-1b\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--per_device_batch_size\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\", type=int, default=4)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--learning_rate\", type=float, default=1e-5)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--spectral_num_samples\", type=int, default=50_000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gen_temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gen_top_p\", type=float, default=0.95)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--no_wandb\", action=\"store_true\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    return parser.parse_args()\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    config = PilotConfig(\n        output_dir=args.output_dir,\n        num_generations=args.num_generations,\n        tokens_per_generation=args.tokens_per_generation,\n        checkpoint_interval_tokens=args.checkpoint_interval_tokens,\n        model_name=args.model_name,\n        per_device_batch_size=args.per_device_batch_size,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\n        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,\n        spectral_num_samples=args.spectral_num_samples,\n        gen_temperature=args.gen_temperature,\n        gen_top_p=args.gen_top_p,\n        use_wandb=not args.no_wandb,\n        seed=args.seed,\n    )\n    run_pilot(config)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF\necho \"pilot_pipeline.py done\""
```json
}
```

### Assistant

_source line 148_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The proxy doesn't seem to be working for the test_gpu.py script. Looking at the log, there are a lot of null bytes at the beginning, and then the error shows that HuggingFace is still unreachable. The `source /etc/network_turbo` is setting `http_proxy` and `https_proxy`, but the model cache already exists at `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/hub/models--EleutherAI--pythia-1b`.

The issue is that even though the model is cached, `AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained` tries to check the remote first. 

I see the config.json loaded successfully based on the torch_dtype warning, but the model.safetensors HEAD request is failing and retrying multiple times instead of falling back to the cache. The real fix here is to set `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` or `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1` to force the script to use only the cached model without attempting any network access.
```

</details>

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The OFFLINE mode is set but it's still trying to connect. The issue is that the `torch_dtype` deprecation warning appeared, which means the config was loaded from cache, but it's still trying to check for the model weights. This could be a bug in the transformers version.

Wait, I see: the null bytes (\u0000) suggest a progress bar being overwritten. And the retry messages say the connection fails. But with `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1`, it should not attempt network connections at all.

Looking again - the error is "Network is unreachable". The proxy variables are set but they're still unreachable. 

The null bytes in the output suggest that `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` might not be properly recognized by this version of transformers - it's loading the config from cache but still attempting to fetch the model file. Let me verify the model snapshot is actually complete in the cache.
```

</details>

```text
Wait, the curl returns 200, meaning the proxy works. But the Python process gets "Network is unreachable". This means the proxy env vars are NOT being inherited by the Python process when using nohup.

Let me check - in run_test.sh, I do `source /etc/network_turbo` which sets `http_proxy` and `https_proxy`. 

The `no_proxy` settings might be interfering with how the requests library picks up the proxy configuration. But I also set `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` which should prevent network access entirely - yet the logs still show HEAD requests happening. Either the environment variable isn't being passed through properly, or this version of huggingface_hub doesn't respect that flag. Let me examine the snapshot more carefully and try forcing `local_files_only=True` directly in the Python code instead.
```

### Assistant

_source line 168_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
The BATCH_TEST_PASS appeared! The output says:
```
Loading model from cache...
Model loaded, running forward pass...
OK. GPU mem: 6.5GB / 95.0GB
BATCH_TEST_PASS
```

GPU batch test passed: 6.5GB used out of 95.0GB with batch size 8. 

But looking more carefully, this was actually the SECOND test run (PID 6006, started earlier). The new one (PID 6279) was just started and the log was overwritten. Actually wait, I see the old HuggingFace retry errors at the bottom too. Let me look more carefully:


I'm seeing the successful test output followed by null bytes and retry errors, which are confusing me about which run produced what. The log file should have been overwritten with the redirect, but nohup appends by default, so I'm getting mixed output from both runs in the same file.

The important thing is that BATCH_TEST_PASS confirms the GPU test succeeded with 6.5GB memory usage on batch size 8 and sequence length 2048. Now I need to ensure the actual pipeline run also respects the HF_HOME environment variable and handles offline model loading properly, since the pipeline code doesn't currently use `local_files_only=True` and will try to connect to HuggingFace to check model versions.

The successful run used `local_files_only=True` which loaded from cache, but I'm realizing the proxy setup via `/etc/network_turbo` in the wrapper script didn't actually enable Python to connect to HuggingFace—the successful test only worked because it forced offline mode. For the dry-run pipeline, I should set `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` to ensure it uses only cached models rather than relying on proxy connectivity that may not work reliably. I need to verify that Python actually picks up the proxy settings when the wrapper script runs the dry-run.
````
